{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"claps","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He summoned the waiter with a clap.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of striking the palms of the hands, or any two surfaces, together."]},{"glosses":["The explosive sound of thunder."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Off in the distance, he heard the clap of thunder.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any loud, sudden, explosive sound made by striking hard surfaces together, or resembling such a sound."]},{"examples":[{"text":"His father's affection never went further than a handshake or a clap on the shoulder.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A slap with the hand, usually in a jovial manner."]},{"glosses":["A single, sudden act or motion; a stroke; a blow."]},{"glosses":["The nether part of the beak of a hawk."],"topics":["falconry","hobbies","hunting","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["A dropping of cow dung (presumably from the sound made as it hits the ground)"],"tags":["Yorkshire"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/klæp/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"sound of thunder","word":"thunderclap"}],"upstream_line":151848,"upstream_offset":691366488,"upstream_record_sha256":"7163aae5b687f9e9c4f319fb21b5744cab045b15573ca8e3dbcd4db27593f87b","word":"clap"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"claps","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"clapping","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"clapped","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"clapped","tags":["past"]},{"form":"clapt","tags":["archaic","participle","past"]},{"form":"clapt","tags":["archaic","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The children began to clap in time with the music.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To strike the palms of the hands together, creating a sharp sound."],"synonyms":[{"word":"applaud"},{"word":"beclap"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The audience loudly clapped the actress, who responded with a deep curtsey.","type":"example"},{"text":"It isn’t the singers they are clapping; it's the composer.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To applaud."],"synonyms":[{"word":"acclaim"},{"word":"applaud"},{"word":"hurrah"},{"word":"acclaim"},{"tags":["rare"],"word":"acclamate"},{"word":"applaud"},{"word":"hail"},{"tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"],"word":"barrack"},{"word":"beclap"},{"word":"cheer"},{"word":"cheer on"},{"word":"clap"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"encheer"},{"word":"hurrah"},{"word":"hurray"},{"word":"huzzah"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He would often clap his teammates on the back for encouragement.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To slap with the hand in a jovial manner."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He clapped the empty glass down on the table.","type":"example"},{"text":"She clapped the book shut.","type":"example"},{"text":"He clapped across the floor in his boots.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To bring two surfaces together forcefully, creating a sharp sound."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["To slam (a door or window); formerly often construed with to."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We should clap together a shelter before nightfall.","type":"example"},{"text":"The rival factions clapped up a truce.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To create or assemble (something) hastily (usually followed by up or together)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The sheriff clapped him in jail.","type":"example"},{"text":"She was the prettiest thing I'd ever clapped eyes on.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To set or put, usually in haste."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To shoot (somebody) with a gun."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He started a fight but then got clapped immediately.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To defeat."],"tags":["broadly","slang","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To have sex, fornicate, copulate."],"tags":["slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/klæp/"}],"upstream_line":151849,"upstream_offset":691385033,"upstream_record_sha256":"e6153ba8250c378fd9538b7fdc8352060f0ec50958666c3e8742128af4b4a950","word":"clap"}
